URL Codec and Text Diff follow-up: Text Diff's two-line replacement now has an exact expected unified patch; Copy and `.patch` Download match it byte-for-byte, and Compare/Copy/Download fit the shared dock within the three-button cap. URL Codec's Unicode Copy and text Download both match the encoded output. The approved fix clears result actions and unregisters copy/export capabilities when the mode/input changes or decoding fails. The shared action bar now uses the selected tool's registry title; the full 62-route matrix passes across desktop/390px and light/dark (248 states). Network Diagnostics sweep: desktop/compact WebKit passed DNS lookup, localhost ping and interface listing; the test confirms requests occur on explicit user action, private IP display starts hidden and refresh/copy/download stay within three actions. Copy text and TXT downloads are byte-identical for DNS, ping and interface reports; hidden-interface exports retain the privacy notice and omit IPv4 addresses. The fixture produced no interface addresses, so the explicit reveal screenshots contain no private IPs. Screenshots: `docs/verification/tool-unification/{before,after}/network_diagnostics-*` and `network_{dns,ping,interfaces}-*`. Regression: `ui-tests/network-diagnostics.spec.mjs` (2 passed). Native network permissions and installed-app behavior remain unverified.
Media Toolkit output follow-up: saved an unmet-target WebP result through the app's native `result_save` bridge and checked the suggested filename and artifact bytes exactly; desktop and compact passed 2/2. This verifies the result remains saveable even when the target size is missed, and the bottom bar offers Start over, Save, and Run again (three actions). After screenshots include `media_toolkit-target-saved-{desktop,compact}-webkit.png`.
Compress Images follow-up: selected a disposable JPEG through the native file-grant test seam, ran the real optimizer, fetched its returned artifact and saved it through the UI native bridge. The suggested filename and base64-decoded save bytes match the artifact exactly; source bytes remain unchanged. Desktop and compact passed 2/2, with initial, selected, result and saved screenshots in both evidence folders.
PDF output follow-up: Watermark PDF, Add PDF Page Numbers, Resize PDF Pages, Grayscale PDF and Flatten PDF now have initial captures in `before/` plus ready/preview/saved captures in `after/` for desktop and compact. Real output validity, page counts/text where relevant, unchanged source files, resize-scale/method response headers and the action cap pass across all ten focused scenarios.
File and archive sweep: `OFFICEKIT_EVIDENCE_STAGE=after npx playwright test ui-tests/workspace-files.spec.mjs --project=desktop-webkit --project=compact-webkit` passed all 46 scenarios (23 per layout). This covers Split/Join, ZIP creation/extraction, archive inspection, checksums (SHA-256 and SHA-512), content-based type detection, file/folder reports, comparison, rename previews, duplicate selection/pagination/trash confirmation, empty-file/folder and symlink reports, and Trash restore. The initial run found two test assumption issues, not product defects: compact intentionally hides Date modified sorting, and the async SHA-512 Download assertion read the previous saved fixture before the new save completed. Both tests were corrected and the complete suite passed. Relevant before/after source screenshots are stored in `docs/verification/tool-unification/{before,after}/`; after currently contains 39 captures for this family. Native chooser, Trash, broader keyboard and installed behavior remain open.
Finance documents sweep: Invoices and Bank Statement Extractor ran 22 scenarios across desktop/compact: 20 passed and 2 optional user-sample cases skipped. Statement extraction/reconciliation and long result table behavior passed; the end-to-end invoice flow passed profile/catalogue editing, issue/PDF signature, partial payment, reports, history, backup and restore. The first run exposed a stale test expectation for the shared action bar title; it now asserts the registry title “Invoices” and passes. Captures are stored in `docs/verification/tool-unification/{before,after}/` (`invoice_manager-*`, `bank_statement-*`). Native file chooser and installed behavior remain open.

Full browser-independent JS suite now passes: `node --test tests/*.test.mjs` reports 1,114 passed, 0 failed and 1 skipped across 1,115 tests. This rerun also resolved three harness failures: the calculator error-copy checks now match the optional options parameter, and the PDF shell fixture supplies `pdfOutputBlob` and `Blob` globals that the live app has. Browser-level flows and native/installed-app validation remain separate requirements.
Shared tool baseline rerun: `ui-tests/tool-unification-baseline.spec.mjs` passed 124/124 desktop/compact cases before the Text Diff stale-state fix. Its focused Text Diff regression was rerun afterward and passes 2/2. The baseline covers registered representative workflows, invalid input recovery, output agreement, favorites/recents and stale-result behavior. This is source WebKit evidence; installed-app checks remain a separate open item.

Voice Transcription functional pass: a native-bridge fixture returns a known transcript and English translation. Desktop and compact WebKit both verify exact editor/translation text, the stage-appropriate three-action bottom bar, audio-file history source, exact history Copy and saved TXT bytes, plus model-error recovery without losing the transcript (2/2). Before/import/error screenshots: `docs/verification/tool-unification/after/voice_transcription-{before-audio,imported,model-error-recovery}-{desktop,compact}-webkit.png`. This is a deterministic bridge fixture; an actual user audio sample and installed microphone/model execution remain open.

Voice Transcription decoder coverage exercises a valid 16 kHz WAV through macOS `afconvert`, finite/non-silent mono samples, empty/unsupported/malformed input, and symlink rejection. This host's `afconvert` rejects MP3 input, so the Rust decoder now falls back to a local FFmpeg executable in known system/Homebrew locations. The fallback restricts protocols to local file/pipe, enforces a 120-second timeout and five-minute decoded-size cap, then validates mono float WAV samples. The bundled MP3 fixture passes through the fallback (`cargo test --manifest-path src-tauri/Cargo.toml local_audio_decoder`: 3 passed). FFmpeg remains optional and is not bundled; MP3 fallback on a clean install without FFmpeg and model-backed/installed-app transcription remain unverified.

## Initial action-bar exceptions to verify

The 62-route matrix checks each route's initial state, so these results are provisional until the interactive workflow is exercised:

- Scientific Calculator is a live-input calculator; its result actions appear after a valid expression is entered. It should be checked in both empty and result states, not given an empty persistent bar. Unit Converter now produces its valid default result after the first asynchronous catalog load; regression coverage prevents first-entry actions from disappearing.
- Speech Coach is a preference and feedback surface. Its switches and per-insight feedback are contextual controls rather than a single primary task.
- Clipboard & Snippets Studio is a multi-item workspace; actions depend on the selected item/clipboard state.
- Global Launcher is a shortcut/configuration surface, not a one-shot tool operation.
- PDF annotation and screenshot annotation use dense, task-specific canvas toolbars because controls change the document directly (selection, drawing/annotation modes, zoom, page movement, undo/redo). Keep those focused tool palettes beside the canvas; keep final actions such as Save/Download in the shared bottom bar. Screenshot Editor evidence shows Save and More in the bottom bar with PNG format selection; its editing controls stay in the canvas toolbar and remain reachable while scrolling (`ui-tests/screenshot-editor.spec.mjs`, 28 desktop/compact cases).
- Media Toolkit is a multi-step workspace, but it still follows the shared bottom-bar pattern. Its footer groups Start over, Save, and the current Run action; the action label changes with operation and file count. Keep operation/result format controls with their settings and reserve the footer for current workflow actions. Desktop/compact evidence: `docs/verification/tool-unification/after/media_toolkit-target-result-{desktop,compact}-webkit.png`; the action and button-cap scenarios live in `ui-tests/media-toolkit.spec.mjs`.
- These are contextual workspaces rather than exceptions to a shared action location: annotation tool palettes remain canvas-specific, while durable output or run actions remain in the same bottom bar as other tools.

2026-10-30 Media Toolkit audio-normalize output recheck: the local normalizer processed the 1-second `audio-tone.mp3` fixture into an AAC/M4A copy, reported −18 LUFS within 1 LU, met `target_met`, and measured true peak at or below the −2 dBFS ceiling. FFprobe confirmed mono AAC at 44.1 or 48 kHz and duration within 0.8–1.2 s; FFmpeg decoded the artifact; original fixture bytes stayed unchanged. An initial assertion incorrectly assumed 44.1 kHz; the real adapter outputs 48 kHz, which is valid for this operation, so the regression now accepts both AAC rates while pinning codec, channels, duration, loudness, peak and source preservation. `pytest -q -rs tests/test_media_native_integration.py` passes 2/2.

2026-10-30 Media Toolkit audio-speed output probe: operation availability reports true because the macOS transform helper exists, but both the shipped 1-second MP3 fixture and a generated 0.1-second PCM WAV fail during native M4A export with `AVFoundationErrorDomain -11800`; a direct helper invocation reproduces the failure. 2026-10-30 Media Toolkit audio-speed fix: when the local FFmpeg AAC encoder is available, run audio speed through bounded `atempo` filter chains instead of relying on the failing AVFoundation export. 2026-10-30 Screenshot Editor macOS clipboard fix: the reported arboard `ContentNotAvailable` came from its macOS adapter requesting only `NSPasteboardTypeTIFF`; PNG-only pasteboards were therefore treated as empty. Added the `screenshot_clipboard_read` native command to read bounded PNG first and TIFF second, then decode through the existing bounded browser normalizer. If WebKit denies clipboard read, the page now reports an empty/unreadable clipboard and leaves keyboard paste/file selection available instead of surfacing the raw permission exception. Regressions cover PNG-only native fallback and WebKit-denied read. `node --test tests/screenshot-editor-ui.test.mjs` passes 15/15; `cargo check --manifest-path src-tauri/Cargo.toml`, `cargo build --manifest-path src-tauri/Cargo.toml`, `cargo fmt --check`, UI build and `git diff --check` pass. The currently open `target/debug/officekit` process predates the native command and CUA still cannot attach (`cgWindowNotFound`), so the actual OS clipboard path needs confirmation after relaunch; this fix is implemented but installed/native runtime is not yet marked passed. Action-bar/canvas layout is unchanged and remains the documented exception.
